WczeÅ›niej kwadratowa twarz byÅ‚a rozciÄ…gana do pionowego bannera, przez co nawet dobry portret wyglÄ…daÅ‚ nienaturalnie. */ function drawCrestImageContained(ctx,img,x,y,w,h,pad){ if(!ctx||!img||w<=0||h<=0)return; var dims=imageSourceSize(img),iw=dims.w,ih=dims.h; if(!iw||!ih)return; pad=Math.max(0,pad||0); var aw=Math.max(1,w-pad*2),ah=Math.max(1,h-pad*2); var scale=Math.min(aw/iw,ah/ih),dw=iw*scale,dh=ih*scale; ctx.drawImage(img,x+(w-dw)/2,y+(h-dh)/2,dw,dh); } function clampFaceBox(box,w,h){ if(!box||!w||!h)return null; var x=Number(box.x!==undefined?box.x:box.left)||0; var y=Number(box.y!==undefined?box.y:box.top)||0; var bw=Math.max(1,Number(box.width)||0),bh=Math.max(1,Number(box.height)||0); x=Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x));y=Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y)); bw=Math.max(1,Math.min(w-x,bw));bh=Math.max(1,Math.min(h-y,bh)); return {x:x,y:y,width:bw,height:bh}; } function fallbackPortraitFaceBox(source){ var dims=imageSourceSize(source),w=dims.w,h=dims.h; if(!w||!h)return null; var portrait=h>w; var fw=Math.min(w*(portrait ? .58 : .42),h*(portrait ? .42 : .52)); var fh=fw*1.16; var cy=h*(portrait ? .32 : .40); return clampFaceBox({x:w*.5-fw*.5,y:cy-fh*.5,width:fw,height:fh},w,h); } /* MaÅ‚y, caÅ‚kowicie lokalny tryb zapasowy dla przeglÄ…darek bez FaceDetector. Szuka spÃ³jnego obszaru odcieni skÃ³ry, preferujÄ…c twarz blisko Å›rodka zdjÄ™cia. */ function detectFaceBySkin(source){ try{ var dims=imageSourceSize(source),w=dims.w,h=dims.h; if(!w||!h)return null; var maxSide=128,scale=Math.min(1,maxSide/Math.max(w,h)); var sw=Math.max(24,Math.round(w*scale)),sh=Math.max(24,Math.round(h*scale)); var scan=document.createElement("canvas");scan.width=sw;scan.height=sh; var sctx=scan.getContext("2d",{willReadFrequently:true}); sctx.drawImage(source,0,0,sw,sh); var pixels=sctx.getImageData(0,0,sw,sh).data,total=sw*sh; var skin=new Uint8Array(total),seen=new Uint8Array(total); for(var i=0;i38&&spread>10&&cb>72&&cb<138&&cr>126&&cr<188&&r>g*.82&&r>b*.88)skin[i]=1; } var queue=new Int32Array(total),best=null,minCount=Math.max(18,Math.round(total*.0025)); for(var start=0;startmaxX)maxX=px;if(pymaxY)maxY=py; var n; if(px>0){n=p-1;if(skin[n]&&!seen[n]){seen[n]=1;queue[qt++]=n;}} if(px0){n=p-sw;if(skin[n]&&!seen[n]){seen[n]=1;queue[qt++]=n;}} if(py1.9||boxArea/total>.34||count/boxArea<.16)continue; var cx=(minX+maxX)*.5/sw,cy=(minY+maxY)*.5/sh; var central=1-Math.min(1,Math.hypot((cx-.5)*1.45,(cy-.40)*1.05)); var faceShape=1-Math.min(1,Math.abs(ratio-.78)); var score=count*(.55+central*.8+faceShape*.35); if(!best||score>best.score)best={x:minX,y:minY,width:bw,height:bh,score:score}; } if(!best)return null; return clampFaceBox({x:best.x/scale,y:best.y/scale,width:best.width/scale,height:best.height/scale},w,h); }catch(err){return null;} } function makeFaceDetectionCanvas(source){ var dims=imageSourceSize(source),scale=Math.min(1,FACE_DETECT_MAX/Math.max(dims.w,dims.h)); var canvas=document.createElement("canvas"); canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(dims.w*scale));canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(dims.h*scale)); var ctx=canvas.getContext("2d");ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality="high"; ctx.drawImage(source,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height); return {canvas:canvas,scale:scale}; } function detectLargestFace(source){ var dims=imageSourceSize(source); if(typeof FaceDetector!=="function")return Promise.resolve(null); try{ var det=makeFaceDetectionCanvas(source); var detector=new FaceDetector({fastMode:true,maxDetectedFaces:6}); return detector.detect(det.canvas).then(function(faces){ if(!faces||!faces.length)return null; var best=null,bestScore=-1; for(var i=0;ibestScore){bestScore=score;best=box;} } return best?clampFaceBox({x:best.x/det.scale,y:best.y/det.scale,width:best.width/det.scale,height:best.height/det.scale},dims.w,dims.h):null; }).catch(function(){return null;}); }catch(err){return Promise.resolve(null);} } /* WyjÅ›ciowy PNG zachowuje prawdziwe kolory zdjÄ™cia. Zmieniany jest wyÅ‚Ä…cznie kadr i kanaÅ‚ alfa na miÄ™kkiej owalnej krawÄ™dzi â€” bez cartoon i kwantyzacji. */ function createFaceCutoutCanvas(source,faceBox){ var dims=imageSourceSize(source),w=dims.w,h=dims.h,size=CREST_MAX_SIZE; var box=clampFaceBox(faceBox,w,h)||fallbackPortraitFaceBox(source); var cropSide=Math.max(box.width*1.58,box.height*1.42); cropSide=Math.max(8,Math.min(Math.max(w,h),cropSide)); var cx=box.x+box.width*.5,cy=box.y+box.height*.49; var sx=cx-cropSide*.5,sy=cy-cropSide*.49; var srcX=Math.max(0,sx),srcY=Math.max(0,sy); var srcR=Math.min(w,sx+cropSide),srcB=Math.min(h,sy+cropSide); var srcW=Math.max(1,srcR-srcX),srcH=Math.max(1,srcB-srcY); var dx=(srcX-sx)/cropSide*size,dy=(srcY-sy)/cropSide*size; var dw=srcW/cropSide*size,dh=srcH/cropSide*size; var out=document.createElement("canvas");out.width=size;out.height=size; var ctx=out.getContext("2d");ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality="high"; ctx.drawImage(source,srcX,srcY,srcW,srcH,dx,dy,dw,dh); var maskCx=(box.x+box.width*.5-sx)/cropSide*size; var maskCy=(box.y+box.height*.5-sy)/cropSide*size; var maskRx=Math.min(size*.47,box.width/cropSide*size*.64); var maskRy=Math.min(size*.52,box.height/cropSide*size*.64); maskRx=Math.max(size*.31,maskRx);maskRy=Math.max(size*.39,maskRy); var mask=document.createElement("canvas");mask.width=size;mask.height=size; var m=mask.getContext("2d");m.save();m.translate(maskCx,maskCy);m.scale(1,maskRy/maskRx); var grad=m.createRadialGradient(0,0,maskRx*.78,0,0,maskRx); grad.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,1)");grad.addColorStop(.78,"rgba(255,255,255,1)");grad.addColorStop(1,"rgba(255,255,255,0)"); m.fillStyle=grad;m.fillRect(-size,-size,size*2,size*2);m.restore(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation="destination-in";ctx.drawImage(mask,0,0);ctx.globalCompositeOperation="source-over"; return out; } function extractFaceImage(source){ return detectLargestFace(source).then(function(nativeBox){ var skinBox=nativeBox?null:detectFaceBySkin(source); var box=nativeBox||skinBox||fallbackPortraitFaceBox(source); return {canvas:createFaceCutoutCanvas(source,box),box:box,method:nativeBox?"native":(skinBox?"skin":"portrait")}; }); } function setCrestBusy(isP,busy){ var btn=document.getElementById(isP?"crestLoadP":"crestLoadE"); if(!btn)return; btn.disabled=!!busy;btn.textContent=busy?tr("menu.faceProcessing"):tr("menu.upload"); } function loadCrestFromStorage(){ try{ var pData=localStorage.getItem("castleCalamityCrestP")||""; } if(pData && pData.startsWith("data:image")){ var imgP = new Image(); imgP.onload = function(){ PLAYER_CREST = imgP; renderCrestPreview("crestPreviewP", PLAYER_CREST, true); }; imgP.onerror = function(){ console.warn("Failed to load player crest");
